
MONOPOLY-LK Simulation

Player 1 : Aggressive Invester
Player 2 : Conservative Banker
Player 3 : Risk Taker
Player 4 : Oppertunistic Trader

Each Player begins with LKR 30,000.

Aggressive Invester rolls 4.
Conservative Banker rolls 5.
Risk Taker rolls 5.
Oppertunistic Trader rolls 9.

There is a tie between the following players:
- Conservative Banker
- Risk Taker
will roll again

Conservative Banker rolls 7.
Risk Taker rolls 7.

There is a tie between the following players:
- Conservative Banker
- Risk Taker
will roll again

Conservative Banker rolls 2.
Risk Taker rolls 11.

Oppertunistic Trader will begin the game.

Turn Order:
Oppertunistic Trader
Risk Taker
Conservative Banker
Aggressive Invester

Oppertunistic Trader rolled 3+4 = 7.
Oppertunistic Trader moves from Square 0 to Square 7.
Oppertunistic Trader landed on National Event Card 1.
